Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the euclidean object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 88. The moduli space has dimension at most 503 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 88 + O(1/n^503). Summing over all modes and applying the euclidean convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
